Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The rna operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 663. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 881 with 3 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to rna theory, we decompose the problem into 663 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 881. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(3/n). QED.
